# Skills · A · part 28

911 entries, aos-rebuttal to api-doc-generator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **aos-rebuttal**: Use when an Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) revise-and-resubmit arrives — planning revisions that may include recoding field data, new experimental conditions, or deepened theorizing, and drafting the point-by-point response to an interdisciplinary reviewer panel. Drafts the response; it does not run new analysis (aos-data-analysis) or the final preflight (aos-submission). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)
- **aos-review-process**: Use when you need to understand how Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) evaluates a manuscript — triage by the joint Editors-in-Chief, double-anonymized review by interdisciplinary referees, the meaning of each decision type, and how to read the editor's letter. Explains the process; it does not draft the revision response (aos-rebuttal). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-review-process) · ★1.1k)
- **aos-setup**: Bring a machine to a complete, verified AOS installation: the skills pack, MCP servers, gate hooks, memB (engine, MCP, ambient hook, WebUI), OpenWiki (CLI, auth, harness integrations, refresh daemon) and Synapse. Run once on a new computer, and again whenever something in the ecosystem stopped working. ([hybridlabor-api/aos](https://github.com/hybridlabor-api/aos/tree/HEAD/skills/global_config/aos-setup) · ★6)
- **aos-sqlite**: GitHub repository skill for permaweb/aos-sqlite ([enuno/claude-command-and-control](https://github.com/enuno/claude-command-and-control/tree/HEAD/skills-templates/aos-sqlite) · ★15)
- **aos-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for an Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) manuscript — Editorial Manager files (anonymized manuscript, separate title page, declarations), Elsevier policies (competing interests, AI, ethics), and open-access decisions. Checks readiness to submit; it does not handle the post-decision response (aos-rebuttal). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **aos-tables-figures**: Use when building the exhibits of an Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) manuscript — data-inventory and evidence tables for qualitative work, cell-means and process-test tables for experiments, and figures that carry theoretical weight — in Elsevier-compatible format. Builds exhibits; it does not run the analysis (aos-data-analysis). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-tables-figures) · ★1.1k)
- **aos-theory-development**: Use when building the theoretical engine of an Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) manuscript — choosing a social/organizational theory tradition (institutional theory, governmentality, Bourdieu, actor-network theory, practice theory, psychological process theory) and making it generate the analysis rather than decorate it. Builds the lens; it does not collect data (aos-methods) or frame the contribution (aos-contribution-framing). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-theory-development) · ★1.1k)
- **aos-topic-selection**: Use when choosing or sharpening a research question for an Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) manuscript — testing whether the question genuinely concerns the social, organizational, behavioral or institutional life of accounting, and picking the intellectual tradition the question demands. Screens fit; it does not build the theory (aos-theory-development). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **aos-workflow**: Use when deciding which aos-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing manuscript work from topic selection through rebuttal for an Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) manuscript. Routes — it does not replace — the specialized skills. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-workflow) · ★1.1k)
- **aos-writing-style**: Use when polishing the prose of an Accounting, Organizations and Society (AOS) manuscript — sustained argumentative writing that carries theory and evidence together, a self-contained abstract, disciplined length despite the absence of a hard cap, and full anonymization for double-anonymized review. Polishes prose; it does not run the submission preflight (aos-submission). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Accounting-Organizations-and-Society-Skills/skills/aos-writing-style) · ★1.1k)
